# The Module Playbook

> A build standard for business-grade websites: every module gets real content, a conversion purpose, a mobile layout, keyboard support, reduced-motion behaviour, and an empty/error state before any visual layer is added.

**20 sections · 344 modules · Starter 50 · Growth 127 · Business 136 · Flagship 31 · 23 tracked events · every module with code**

---

## How to read an entry

| Field | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| **Type** | The lowest pricing tier the module belongs to: Starter, Growth, Business or Flagship. |
| **File** | The path the module occupies in the app. |
| **Core features** | The verbatim capability list from the source taxonomy. |
| **Purpose** | What the module is, and when it is the right choice. |
| **Rule** | The implementation pitfall that actually bites in production. |
| **Accessibility** | The non-negotiable obligation, where one is specific to the module. |
| **Code** | The reference implementation for that module, as a complete component file. |

Three kinds of code block appear below, and each entry names which it is:

- **reference implementation** — the clean teaching version, running in the component kit.
- **live in this app** — the actual file serving the page you are reading.
- **generated scaffold** — the structure, props, states and keyboard handling the module needs, with the entry's rule and feature list applied as comments; fill in the business copy and data.

## 01 · Global system modules

These appear across nearly every site.

**Architecture**

- Mount these once in the root layout. A header rendered per page breaks focus order and doubles every fetch.
- Header, footer and consent are singletons: one navigation config, one CTA definition, one legal list — desktop and mobile read the same source so they never drift.
- Keep interaction state local (open/closed) and lift only what must persist: theme, consent, locale, dismissed announcements.

**Accessibility**

- The skip link is the first focusable element on the page; the header comes after it.
- Dropdowns and drawers open on focus, close on Escape and outside click, and return focus to the trigger.
- Every icon-only control needs an aria-label and a focus ring that reaches 3:1 against its surface.

**Performance**

- The header is layout-critical: inline its styles, keep the logo SVG under a few kilobytes, and load the search index only when the overlay opens.
- Consent, analytics and chat scripts load after interaction — never in the critical path.

**Quality gate**

- Traverse the whole header and footer with a keyboard only, at 200% zoom.
- Check the sticky bar at 320px width with iOS safe-area insets applied.

### Global system modules — Starter tier (9)

#### SiteHeader

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/layout/SiteHeader.jsx`
- **Core features:** Logo · primary nav · dropdown/mega menu · search · phone · login · language · theme · primary CTA
- **Purpose:** The persistent wayfinding shell: identity, routes, utilities and the single primary action.
- **Rule:** One row, one job per cluster. Below the nav breakpoint collapse into MobileNav instead of letting items wrap.
- **Accessibility:** A <header> landmark containing one <nav aria-label="Primary">.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “SiteHeader — one row, three clusters”
- **Note:** Skip link first, then a landmark header. Nav state is local; the CTA is the only filled control.

```jsx
export function SiteHeader({ nav, onSearch }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(null);
  return (
    <>
      <a
        href="#main"
        className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:left-4 focus:top-4
                   focus:z-50 focus:rounded-sm focus:bg-primary focus:px-4
                   focus:py-2 focus:text-primary-foreground"
      >
        Skip to content
      </a>

      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border bg-background/85 backdrop-blur-xl">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-[140rem] items-center gap-8 px-5 lg:px-10">
          <a href="/" className="font-heading text-lg tracking-tight">Studio</a>

          <nav aria-label="Primary" className="hidden lg:block">
            <ul className="flex items-center gap-7">
              {nav.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.label}>
                  <button
                    aria-expanded={open === item.label}
                    aria-haspopup={item.children ? "true" : undefined}
                    onClick={() => setOpen(open === item.label ? null : item.label)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Escape" && setOpen(null)}
                    className="py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                  >
                    {item.label}
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </nav>

          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={onSearch} aria-label="Search" className="rounded-sm p-2 hover:bg-secondary">
              <SearchIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
            <a
              href="/book"
              className="rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground"
            >
              Book a call
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>
    </>
  );
}
```

#### MobileNav

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/layout/MobileNav.jsx`
- **Core features:** Full-screen or drawer menu · accordion groups · sticky CTA · contact shortcuts
- **Purpose:** The only navigation most visitors will ever see, and the one most often neglected.
- **Rule:** Trap focus, lock body scroll, and keep the primary CTA pinned above the safe-area inset.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “MobileNav — drawer with a trapped focus”
- **Note:** The drawer is the primary navigation on phones: accordion groups, pinned CTA, safe-area padding.

```jsx
export function MobileNav({ nav, phone }) {
  return (
    <Sheet>
      <SheetTrigger asChild>
        <button aria-label="Open menu" className="lg:hidden">
          <MenuIcon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </SheetTrigger>

      <SheetContent
        side="right"
        className="flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col p-0 sm:w-96"
      >
        <SheetHeader className="border-b border-border px-5 py-4 text-left">
          <SheetTitle className="font-heading text-lg">Menu</SheetTitle>
        </SheetHeader>

        <nav aria-label="Mobile" className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-6">
          <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
            {nav.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.label} className="py-1">
                <details>
                  <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none py-3 text-base">
                    {item.label}
                  </summary>
                  <ul className="pb-3 pl-3">
                    {item.children?.map((child) => (
                      <li key={child.href}>
                        <a href={child.href} className="block py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                          {child.label}
                        </a>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </details>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>

        <div
          className="border-t border-border p-5"
          style={{ paddingBottom: "calc(1.25rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))" }}
        >
          <a href="/book" className="block rounded-full bg-primary py-3 text-center text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground">
            Book a call
          </a>
          <a href={"tel:" + phone} className="mt-3 block text-center font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {phone}
          </a>
        </div>
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

#### SiteFooter

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/layout/SiteFooter.jsx`
- **Core features:** NAP · sitemap · social · legal · newsletter · certifications · locations · app links
- **Purpose:** The catch-all: identity, contact, legal and a compact second sitemap for crawlers and humans.
- **Rule:** Repeat the primary CTA here, but link groups rather than every child route — a footer is not a sitemap dump.
- **Code:** live in this app

```jsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { primaryNav } from "@/lib/nav";
import { categories, moduleCount } from "@/lib/playbook";

export default function SiteFooter() {
  return (
    <footer className="border-t border-border bg-card/40">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[140rem] px-5 py-16 lg:px-10">
        <div className="grid gap-12 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr_1fr_1fr]">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 bg-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-semibold tracking-tight">Module Playbook</span>
            </div>
            <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
              A build standard for business-grade websites: {categories.length} sections, {moduleCount} modules,
              each with its purpose, build rules and an implementation note.
            </p>
          </div>

          <nav aria-label="Playbook">
            <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Library</p>
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm">
              {primaryNav.slice(0, 3).map((item) => (
                <li key={item.to}>
                  <Link to={item.to} className="text-foreground/80 hover:text-primary">
                    {item.label}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </nav>

          <nav aria-label="Reference">
            <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Reference</p>
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm">
              {primaryNav[3].children.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.to}>
                  <Link to={item.to} className="text-foreground/80 hover:text-primary">
                    {item.label}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </nav>

          <div>
            <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Sections</p>
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm">
              {categories.slice(0, 5).map((category) => (
                <li key={category.slug}>
                  <Link
                    to="/categories/$categorySlug"
                    params={{ categorySlug: category.slug }}
                    className="text-foreground/80 hover:text-primary"
                  >
                    {category.title}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-14 flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-border pt-6 text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <p>Motion supports hierarchy. Content survives without it.</p>
          <p className="font-mono uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">Version 1.0 · {moduleCount} modules</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}
```

#### AnnouncementBar

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/global-system/AnnouncementBar.jsx`
- **Core features:** Offer · emergency notice · holiday hours · policy update · event banner
- **Purpose:** One line of time-sensitive context above everything else on the page.
- **Rule:** Dismissible and remembered per message id; never stack two announcements at once.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// AnnouncementBar — global system
// One line of time-sensitive context above everything else on the page.
// Rule: Dismissible and remembered per message id; never stack two announcements at once.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Offer · emergency notice · holiday hours · policy update · event banner
export default function AnnouncementBar({ nav = [], logo = "Brand", ctaLabel, onCta }) {
  return (
    <header className="border-b border-border bg-background">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center gap-6 px-5 lg:px-10">
        <a href="/" className="font-heading text-sm font-semibold">{logo}</a>
        <nav aria-label="Primary" className="ml-auto hidden lg:block">
          <ul className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {nav.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.href}>
                <a href={item.href} aria-current={item.current ? "page" : undefined} className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>
        {ctaLabel ? (
          <button type="button" onClick={onCta} className="ml-auto bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground lg:ml-0">
            {ctaLabel}
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

#### Breadcrumb

- **Purpose:** Shows depth inside a hierarchy and gives a one-click step back up.

- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// Breadcrumb — global system
// Shows depth inside a hierarchy and gives a one-click step back up.
// Rule: Derive it from the real URL hierarchy and pair it with BreadcrumbList structured data.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Current location · parent links · structured-data support
export default function Breadcrumb({ nav = [], logo = "Brand", ctaLabel, onCta }) {
  return (
    <header className="border-b border-border bg-background">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center gap-6 px-5 lg:px-10">

              <li key={item.href}>
                <a href={item.href} aria-current={item.current ? "page" : undefined} className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>
        {ctaLabel ? (
          <button type="button" onClick={onCta} className="ml-auto bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground lg:ml-0">
            {ctaLabel}
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

#### SkipToContent

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/global-system/SkipToContent.jsx`
- **Core features:** Keyboard accessibility
- **Purpose:** The first tab stop, jumping straight past the header into main content.
- **Rule:** Visible on focus, targeting the id on <main>, and working with a sticky header offset.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// SkipToContent — global system
// The first tab stop, jumping straight past the header into main content.
// Rule: Visible on focus, targeting the id on <main>, and working with a sticky header offset.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Keyboard accessibility
export default function SkipToContent({ eyebrow, heading, intro, items = [], action }) {
  return (
    <section className="border-b border-border">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-6xl gap-10 px-5 py-16 lg:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr] lg:px-10">
        <div>
          {eyebrow ? <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-primary">{eyebrow}</p> : null}
          <h2 className="mt-4 font-heading text-[clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)] leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em]">{heading}</h2>
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### CookieConsent

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/system/CookieConsent.jsx`
- **Core features:** Accept/reject/manage · category toggles · policy links
- **Purpose:** Consent capture that actually gates the scripts it claims to gate.
- **Rule:** Reject must be as easy as accept; store the decision with a version and timestamp.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “CookieConsent — reject is as easy as accept”
- **Note:** Store the decision with a version. Gate scripts on the category, not on the banner being closed.

```jsx
const KEY = "consent:v1";

function CookieConsent({ onDecide }) {
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setChoice(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "null"));
    } catch {
      setChoice(null);
    }
  }, []);

  const save = (value) => {
    const record = { ...value, version: 1, at: new Date().toISOString() };
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(record));
    setChoice(record);
    onDecide?.(record);
  };

  if (choice) return null;

  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="Cookie preferences"
         className="fixed inset-x-4 bottom-4 z-50 border border-border bg-card p-5 shadow-2xl md:inset-x-auto md:right-6 md:max-w-md">
      <p className="text-sm text-foreground">
        We use cookies for analytics and to improve the site. Essential cookies are always on.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <button onClick={() => save({ analytics: true })} className="rounded-sm bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground">
          Accept all
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => save({ analytics: false })} className="rounded-sm border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
          Reject optional
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### ErrorBoundary

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/DefaultCatchBoundary.jsx`
- **Core features:** Friendly failure state and recovery action
- **Purpose:** Keeps one broken component from blanking an otherwise working page.
- **Rule:** Offer retry and a route home; log the error without rendering a stack trace to the visitor.
- **Code:** live in this app

```jsx
import { ErrorComponent, Link, rootRouteId, useMatch, useRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function DefaultCatchBoundary({ error }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const isRoot = useMatch({ strict: false, select: (state) => state.id === rootRouteId });

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-2xl flex-col items-center gap-4 py-24">
      <ErrorComponent error={error} />
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.invalidate()}>
          Try again
        </Button>
        <Button asChild>{isRoot ? <Link to="/">Home</Link> : <Link to="/" onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); window.history.back(); }}>Go back</Link>}</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### NotFound

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/NotFound.jsx`
- **Core features:** Helpful 404 with search, popular links, CTA
- **Purpose:** Recovers a dead-end visit into a useful next step.
- **Rule:** Return a real 404 status server-side — a styled page sent with 200 is a soft 404.
- **Code:** live in this app

```jsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function NotFound({ children }) {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-lg flex-col items-center gap-4 py-24 text-center">
      <p className="text-6xl font-semibold tracking-tight">404</p>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground">{children ?? "The page you are looking for does not exist."}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => window.history.back()}>
          Go back
        </Button>
        <Button asChild>
          <Link to="/">Start over</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Global system modules — Growth tier (5)

#### MegaMenu

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/layout/MegaMenu.jsx`
- **Core features:** Columned links · featured page · image · CTA · industry/service shortcuts
- **Purpose:** Exposes a deep route tree — services, industries, documentation — without costing a second click.
- **Rule:** Cap it at four columns and close on route change, restoring focus to the trigger.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “MegaMenu — panel on focus, not on hover only”
- **Note:** Opens on hover and on focus-within, closes on Escape and on route change, and keeps four columns maximum.

```jsx
export function MegaMenu({ group, onNavigate }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => e.key === "Escape" && setOpen(false);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className="relative"
      onMouseEnter={() => setOpen(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setOpen(false)}
      onBlur={(e) => !ref.current.contains(e.relatedTarget) && setOpen(false)}
    >
      <button aria-expanded={open} aria-haspopup="true" onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}>
        {group.label}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label={group.label}
          className="absolute left-0 top-full grid w-[62rem] grid-cols-4 gap-8 border border-border
                     bg-popover p-8 shadow-2xl"
        >
          {group.columns.map((column) => (
            <div key={column.title}>
              <p className="mb-3 font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
                {column.title}
              </p>
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {column.links.map((link) => (
                  <li key={link.href}>
                    <a href={link.href} onClick={onNavigate} className="text-sm hover:text-primary">
                      {link.label}
                    </a>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### StickyActionBar

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/layout/StickyActionBar.jsx`
- **Core features:** Call · book · quote · order · directions · WhatsApp · chat
- **Purpose:** Keeps the dominant conversion action within thumb reach on small screens.

- **Accessibility:** Give <main> a scroll-margin-bottom equal to the bar height.


#### BackToTop

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/layout/BackToTop.jsx`
- **Core features:** Long-page navigation
- **Purpose:** A way out of long documents that doesn't require hunting for the header.
- **Rule:** Appear only after real scroll distance, and honour reduced motion when jumping.
- **Code:** live in this app

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowUp } from "lucide-react";

export default function BackToTop() {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setVisible(window.scrollY > 800);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" })}
      aria-label="Back to top"
      className={
        "fixed bottom-6 right-5 z-30 border border-border bg-card p-3 text-muted-foreground transition-all duration-500 ease-elite hover:border-primary hover:text-primary " +
        (visible ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "pointer-events-none translate-y-3 opacity-0")
      }
    >
      <ArrowUp className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
    </button>
  );
}
```

#### ThemeToggle

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/system/ThemeToggle.jsx`
- **Core features:** Light/dark/high-contrast mode
- **Purpose:** Lets a visitor choose a reading environment that suits the light they are in.
- **Rule:** Apply the stored theme before first paint to avoid a flash, and never signal mode by colour alone.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “ThemeToggle — applied before first paint”
- **Note:** A tiny inline script sets the class in the document head so the stored theme never flashes.

```jsx
// Inline in the document head, before any stylesheet:
// (function () {
//   var stored = localStorage.getItem("theme");
//   var light = stored === "light";
//   document.documentElement.classList.toggle("light", light);
// })();

function ThemeToggle() {
  const [light, setLight] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setLight(document.documentElement.classList.contains("light"));
  }, []);

  const toggle = () => {
    const next = !light;
    setLight(next);
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("light", next);
    localStorage.setItem("theme", next ? "light" : "dark");
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggle}
      aria-pressed={light}
      aria-label={"Switch to " + (light ? "dark" : "light") + " theme"}
      className="rounded-sm p-2 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
    >
      {light ? <MoonIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /> : <SunIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />}
    </button>
  );
}
```

#### SearchOverlay

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/layout/SearchOverlay.jsx`
- **Core features:** Site search · recent searches · suggested pages · filters
- **Purpose:** Search that covers the page instead of a field squeezed into the header.
- **Rule:** Debounce input, hand-write empty and no-result states, and keep recent queries local only.
- **Code:** live in this app

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useRouterState } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Search, CornerDownLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { searchModules, categories } from "@/lib/playbook";

export default function SearchOverlay({ open, onClose }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const pathname = useRouterState({ select: (state) => state.location.pathname });
  const firstRun = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) setQuery("");
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (firstRun.current) {
      firstRun.current = false;
      return;
    }
    onClose();
  }, [pathname, onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const previous = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previous;
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const results = query.trim() ? searchModules(query).slice(0, 8) : [];

  const go = (slug) => {
    onClose();
    navigate({ to: "/modules/$moduleSlug", params: { moduleSlug: slug } });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center bg-background/85 px-4 pt-24 backdrop-blur-md">
      <button className="absolute inset-0 cursor-default" aria-label="Close search" onClick={onClose} />
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Search the playbook"
        className="relative w-full max-w-2xl border border-border bg-card shadow-2xl"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-border px-4">
          <Search className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
          <input
            autoFocus
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "Enter" && results[0]) go(results[0].slug);
            }}
            placeholder="Search 344 modules — try “booking”, “proof”, “menu”"
            aria-label="Search modules"
            className="w-full bg-transparent py-4 text-base outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground"
          />
          <kbd className="hidden shrink-0 border border-border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.6rem] text-muted-foreground sm:block">
            ESC
          </kbd>
        </div>

        {query.trim() === "" ? (
          <div className="p-4">
            <p className="mb-3 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
              Jump to a section
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {categories.slice(0, 10).map((category) => (
                <button
                  key={category.slug}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onClose();
                    navigate({ to: "/categories/$categorySlug", params: { categorySlug: category.slug } });
                  }}
                  className="border border-border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary hover:text-foreground"
                >
                  {category.title}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ul className="max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto py-2">
            {results.map((module) => (
              <li key={module.slug}>
                <button
                  onClick={() => go(module.slug)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-4 px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors hover:bg-secondary"
                >
                  <span className="flex-1">
                    <span className="block text-sm text-foreground">{module.name}</span>
                    <span className="mt-0.5 block truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {module.purpose}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="hidden shrink-0 font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground sm:block">
                    {module.categoryTitle}
                  </span>
                  <CornerDownLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
            {results.length === 0 && (
              <li className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                No module matches “{query}”. Try a broader term such as “form” or “hero”.
              </li>
            )}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### CommandPalette

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/system/CommandPalette.jsx`
- **Core features:** Keyboard-first navigation for large sites
- **Purpose:** Fast navigation for documentation, dashboards and route trees too large for a menu.
- **Rule:** ?K / Ctrl+K to open, arrows to move, Enter to go. Index titles and sections, not page bodies.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “CommandPalette — ?K over a static index”
- **Note:** Index titles and sections only. Arrow keys move, Enter navigates, Escape closes.

```jsx
export function CommandPalette({ items }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setOpen((value) => !value);
      }
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, []);

  const results = items
    .filter((item) => item.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()))
    .slice(0, 8);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Command palette"
         className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="mx-auto mt-24 w-[min(36rem,calc(100vw-2rem))] border border-border bg-card shadow-2xl">
        <input
          autoFocus
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Jump to a module or page"
          className="w-full border-b border-border bg-transparent px-4 py-4 text-base outline-none"
        />
        <ul className="max-h-80 overflow-y-auto py-2">
          {results.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.href}>
              <button
                onClick={() => { navigate(item.href); setOpen(false); }}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-between px-4 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary"
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  {item.group}
                </span>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
          {results.length === 0 && (
            <li className="px-4 py-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing matches “{query}”.</li>
          )}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### ConsentGate

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/global-system/ConsentGate.jsx`
- **Core features:** Blocks third-party scripts until consent
- **Purpose:** Conditional loader for analytics, embeds and marketing pixels.
- **Rule:** Gate the script, not the layout; unmount it when consent is withdrawn, not just on load.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ConsentGate — global system
// Conditional loader for analytics, embeds and marketing pixels.
// Rule: Gate the script, not the layout; unmount it when consent is withdrawn, not just on load.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Blocks third-party scripts until consent
export default function ConsentGate({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

## 02 · Hero and attention modules

Use one hero pattern per page, not five competing effects.

**Architecture**

- A hero makes one decision: what to read first and what to do next. Pick one pattern per template and commit.
- Render the hero on the server — it is the LCP element and often the only thing a crawler weighs.
- Never give two actions equal weight. The secondary CTA is always a link, not a second filled button.

**Accessibility**

- Exactly one <h1> per page. The eyebrow is a paragraph, not a heading.
- Ambient media needs a poster, alt text or aria-hidden, and autoplaying video needs a pause control.
- Contrast is measured against the worst pixel behind the text: add a scrim instead of hoping.

**Performance**

- Reserve the hero's box with an explicit aspect ratio so the LCP image cannot shift the layout.
- Load the hero image eagerly with responsive sources; defer everything below the fold.

**Quality gate**

- Check the hero at 320px, 768px and 1440px before styling anything else on the page.
- Disable images: the headline, subhead and CTA must still stand on their own.

### Hero and attention modules — Starter tier (2)

#### HeroEditorial

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/sections/HeroEditorial.jsx`
- **Core features:** Eyebrow · headline · subhead · CTA · secondary CTA · image · trust strip
- **Purpose:** Type-led hero for brands whose argument is words rather than product shots.
- **Rule:** Keep the headline under about twelve words and let the subhead carry the qualifier.

- **Note:** The eyebrow is a paragraph, the subhead carries the qualifier, and the trust strip sits under the CTA.

```jsx
export function HeroEditorial({ eyebrow, headline, subhead, primary, secondary, image }) {
  return (
    <section className="border-b border-border">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-[140rem] gap-12 px-5 py-20 lg:grid-cols-12 lg:px-10 lg:py-28">
        <div className="lg:col-span-7">
          <p className="font-mono text-[0.7rem] uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-primary">
            {eyebrow}
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-6 max-w-3xl font-heading text-[clamp(2.5rem,6vw,4.5rem)] leading-[1.05] tracking-[-0.03em]">
            {headline}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-6 max-w-xl text-lg leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
            {subhead}
          </p>
          <div className="mt-9 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
            <a href={primary.href} className="rounded-full bg-primary px-7 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground">
              {primary.label}
            </a>
            {secondary && (

                {secondary.label}
```

---

## 03 · Value and explanation modules

These explain what the business does and why it matters.

**Architecture**

- Order carries the argument: proposition ? benefits ? mechanism ? proof ? price. A page that opens with price has no argument.
- Each module answers one question a sceptical buyer would ask. If two answer the same question, delete one.
- Reuse one card primitive across benefit, feature and service grids so the vertical rhythm stays consistent.

**Accessibility**

- Tabs need real tab semantics with arrow-key movement — not clickable divs.
- Pricing and comparison tables need <th scope> headers and must reflow into stacked rows on narrow screens rather than scrolling sideways.

**Performance**

- Accordions and tabs cut DOM cost but also hide your main argument from the first read. Keep the core case visible without interaction.
- Run calculators on the client and only hit the network when the visitor asks for the result by email.

**Quality gate**

- Read the section with JavaScript disabled: is the offer still comprehensible?
- Every price states tax treatment and billing period in the same line as the number.

### Value and explanation modules — Starter tier (5)

#### ValueProposition

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/value/ValueProposition.jsx`
- **Core features:** Problem · outcome · differentiator · proof
- **Purpose:** The four sentences that tell a stranger why this business exists and why it is different.
- **Rule:** Write the problem in the customer's words, then prove the outcome — the differentiator alone proves nothing.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ValueProposition — value and explanation
// The four sentences that tell a stranger why this business exists and why it is different.
// Rule: Write the problem in the customer's words, then prove the outcome — the differentiator alone proves nothing.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Problem · outcome · differentiator · proof
export default function ValueProposition({ eyebrow, heading, intro, items = [], action }) {
  return (
    <section className="border-b border-border">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-6xl gap-10 px-5 py-16 lg:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr] lg:px-10">
        <div>
          {eyebrow ? <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-primary">{eyebrow}</p> : null}
          <h2 className="mt-4 font-heading text-[clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)] leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em]">{heading}</h2>
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### BenefitGrid

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/sections/BenefitGrid.jsx`
- **Core features:** 3–6 outcome-led cards with icons
- **Purpose:** Six cards maximum, each an outcome rather than a feature name.
- **Rule:** Cap at six and write each card's first line as a verb — 'Cut approval time', not 'Approvals'.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “BenefitGrid — six outcomes, no icons for their own sake”
- **Note:** Each card opens with a verb. Numbers are index labels, not decoration.

```jsx
export function BenefitGrid({ items }) {
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-[140rem] px-5 py-20 lg:px-10">
      <div className="grid gap-px bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {items.map((item, index) => (
          <article key={item.title} className="group bg-background p-8 transition-colors hover:bg-card">
            <span className="font-mono text-[0.65rem] text-muted-foreground">
              {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
            </span>
            <h3 className="mt-6 font-heading text-xl tracking-tight">{item.title}</h3>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            <span className="mt-6 block h-px w-10 bg-primary transition-all duration-500 group-hover:w-20" />
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### ServiceCardGrid

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/value/ServiceCardGrid.jsx`
- **Core features:** Service · description · price-from · CTA
- **Purpose:** The core menu of a service business, each card leading to its own page.

- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ServiceCardGrid — value and explanation
// The core menu of a service business, each card leading to its own page.
// Rule: Never show 'price from' without the qualifier that makes it true.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Service · description · price-from · CTA
export default function ServiceCardGrid({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}
            <p className="font-heading text-base">{item.title}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

#### PricingTable

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/sections/PricingTable.jsx`
- **Core features:** Price · billing period · features · popular plan · CTA
- **Purpose:** The pricing decision surface, with one recommended path.
- **Rule:** One plan may be marked popular; three marked plans is three unmarked plans.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “PricingTable — one recommended plan”
- **Note:** Tax caveat sits on the same line as the number; only one plan is marked.

```jsx
export function PricingTable({ plans, annual, onToggle }) {
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-[120rem] px-5 py-20 lg:px-10">
      <div className="mb-10 flex items-center gap-3">
        <button onClick={onToggle} aria-pressed={annual} className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <span className={annual ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-foreground"}>Monthly</span>
          <Switch checked={annual} onCheckedChange={onToggle} aria-label="Bill annually" />
          <span className={annual ? "text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground"}>Annual</span>
        </button>
        <span className="font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-widest text-primary">2 months free</span>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {plans.map((plan) => (
          <article
            key={plan.name}
            className={
              "flex flex-col border p-8 " +
              (plan.featured ? "border-primary bg-card" : "border-border")
            }
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="font-heading text-lg">{plan.name}</h3>
              {plan.featured && (
                <span className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-widest text-primary">Popular</span>
              )}
            </div>
            <p className="mt-6 font-heading text-4xl tracking-tight">
              {annual ? plan.annual : plan.monthly}
              <span className="ml-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {annual ? "/year" : "/month"}
              </span>
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Excl. VAT. Billed {annual ? "yearly" : "monthly"}.</p>

            <ul className="mt-8 flex-1 space-y-3 text-sm">
              {plan.features.map((feature) => (
                <li key={feature} className="flex gap-3 text-muted-foreground">
                  <CheckIcon className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {feature}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>

            <a
              href={plan.href}
              className={
                "mt-8 rounded-full py-3 text-center text-sm font-medium " +
                (plan.featured
                  ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
                  : "border border-border text-foreground hover:bg-secondary")
              }
            >
              {plan.cta}
            </a>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### SolutionTabs

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/value/SolutionTabs.jsx`
- **Core features:** Tabs by audience · industry · need · or goal
- **Purpose:** Splits one product's story across audiences without five separate pages.
- **Rule:** Deep-link each tab (?audience=legal) so sales can share the relevant view.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// SolutionTabs — value and explanation
// Splits one product's story across audiences without five separate pages.
// Rule: Deep-link each tab (?audience=legal) so sales can share the relevant view.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Tabs by audience · industry · need · or goal
export default function SolutionTabs({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### QuizWizard

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/value/QuizWizard.jsx`
- **Core features:** Guided needs assessment and recommended service
- **Purpose:** Turns an uncertain visitor into a routed lead with a recommendation.
- **Rule:** Three to five questions, visible progress, and a result that is genuinely different per answer.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// QuizWizard — value and explanation
// Turns an uncertain visitor into a routed lead with a recommendation.
// Rule: Three to five questions, visible progress, and a result that is genuinely different per answer.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Guided needs assessment and recommended service
export default function QuizWizard({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### ProductTour

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/value/ProductTour.jsx`
- **Core features:** Screenshots · feature callouts · demo video
- **Purpose:** Walks an evaluator through the product before they sign up.
- **Rule:** Annotate real screenshots; re-record when the UI changes or the tour quietly becomes a lie.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ProductTour — value and explanation
// Walks an evaluator through the product before they sign up.
// Rule: Annotate real screenshots; re-record when the UI changes or the tour quietly becomes a lie.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Screenshots · feature callouts · demo video
export default function ProductTour({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### BeforeAfterSlider

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/media/BeforeAfterSlider.jsx`
- **Core features:** Draggable comparison · captions · keyboard control
- **Purpose:** Shows transformation where the change is visual: renovation, retouching, restoration.
- **Rule:** Needs a real keyboard path — arrow keys on a focusable handle — not drag alone.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “BeforeAfterSlider — drag and arrow keys”
- **Note:** A focusable handle with role=slider is the keyboard path; pointer drag is the enhancement.

```jsx
export function BeforeAfterSlider({ before, after, caption }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(50);
  const frame = useRef(null);

  const move = (clientX) => {
    const rect = frame.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    setValue(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100)));
  };

  return (
    <figure>
      <div
        ref={frame}
        className="relative aspect-[16/10] select-none overflow-hidden bg-secondary"
        onPointerMove={(e) => e.buttons === 1 && move(e.clientX)}
      >
        <Image src={after.src} alt={after.alt} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden" style={{ width: value + "%" }}>
          <Image src={before.src} alt={before.alt} className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                 style={{ width: frame.current ? frame.current.offsetWidth + "px" : "100%" }} />
        </div>

        <div
          role="slider"
          tabIndex={0}
          aria-label="Reveal before and after"
          aria-valuemin={0}
          aria-valuemax={100}
          aria-valuenow={Math.round(value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") setValue((v) => Math.max(0, v - 5));
            if (e.key === "ArrowRight") setValue((v) => Math.min(100, v + 5));
          }}
          className="absolute inset-y-0 w-px bg-primary focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
          style={{ left: value + "%" }}
        >
          <span className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-9 w-9 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border border-primary bg-background" />
        </div>
      </div>
      {caption && <figcaption className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption>}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

```jsx
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
```
```jsx
  );
}
```

---

## 04 · Proof and trust modules

Proof should match the claim: metrics for performance claims, named testimonials for service claims, certifications for compliance claims.

**Architecture**

- Proof sits next to the claim it supports, not in a testimonial ghetto at the bottom of the page.
- Never render proof the client cannot substantiate in the CMS: no invented counts, ratings or customer logos.
- Sample or demo content must be labelled — DemoContentBadge is not optional on a template a client will publish.

**Accessibility**

- Star ratings need a text equivalent; never encode the rating in glyphs alone.
- Carousels need a pause control, keyboard arrows, and a first slide that is readable without motion.

**Performance**

- Third-party review widgets are the heaviest element on most pages: load on interaction and reserve their box.
- Serve logos as SVG with explicit dimensions, lazy below the fold.

**Quality gate**

- Every statistic needs a source and a date, visible or linked.
- Check that names, roles and companies are real — or clearly marked as samples.

### Proof and trust modules — Starter tier (3)

#### TrustBar

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/proof/TrustBar.jsx`
- **Core features:** Certifications · partners · guarantees · associations
- **Purpose:** A thin strip of credentials directly under the hero, where doubt first appears.
- **Rule:** Four to six marks maximum; anything unverifiable weakens everything beside it.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// TrustBar — proof and trust
// A thin strip of credentials directly under the hero, where doubt first appears.
// Rule: Four to six marks maximum; anything unverifiable weakens everything beside it.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Certifications · partners · guarantees · associations
export default function TrustBar({ nav = [], logo = "Brand", ctaLabel, onCta }) {
  return (
    <header className="border-b border-border bg-background">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center gap-6 px-5 lg:px-10">
        <a href="/" className="font-heading text-sm font-semibold">{logo}</a>
        <nav aria-label="Primary" className="ml-auto hidden lg:block">
          <ul className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {nav.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.href}>
                <a href={item.href} aria-current={item.current ? "page" : undefined} className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
                  {item.label}
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>
        {ctaLabel ? (
          <button type="button" onClick={onCta} className="ml-auto bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground lg:ml-0">
            {ctaLabel}
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

#### ResultsStatStrip

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/motion/AnimatedCounter.jsx`
- **Core features:** Numeric outcomes with context
- **Purpose:** Three or four numbers that quantify the promise.
- **Rule:** Each number needs its unit, its timeframe and its denominator — '2,400 clients' means nothing without 'since 2019'.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “AnimatedCounter — the number exists without JS”
- **Note:** Render the final value on the server, then animate from zero once visible and only if motion is allowed.

```jsx
export function AnimatedCounter({ value, suffix = "", label }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(value);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduced) return;
    setShown(0);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
      const start = performance.now();
      const tick = (now) => {
        const progress = Math.min(1, (now - start) / 900);
        setShown(Math.round(value * (1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3))));
        if (progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      observer.disconnect();
    }, { threshold: 0.4 });
    if (ref.current) observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [value, reduced]);

  return (
    <div ref={ref}>
      <p className="font-heading text-5xl tracking-tight tabular-nums">
        {shown}
        {suffix}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
```
```jsx
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}

  );
}
```

#### CaseStudyDetail

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/proof/CaseStudyDetail.jsx`
- **Core features:** Narrative · metrics · gallery · process · testimonial
- **Purpose:** The full evidence page a serious buyer forwards internally.
- **Rule:** Structure it as problem ? constraints ? what changed ? measured result, with the metric methodology at the end.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// CaseStudyDetail — proof and trust
// The full evidence page a serious buyer forwards internally.
// Rule: Structure it as problem ? constraints ? what changed ? measured result, with the metric methodology at the end.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Narrative · metrics · gallery · process · testimonial
export default function CaseStudyDetail({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

---

## 05 · Conversion modules

Every template needs one dominant conversion path; secondary actions should support, not compete with it.

**Architecture**

- Model the funnel explicitly: entry point ? capture form or booking ? confirmation route with a reference. One path, one success state.
- Forms post to server code that validates against the same schema the client used. The browser is a convenience, never the gate.
- Persist multi-step drafts so a refresh or a phone call does not destroy progress; expire them deliberately.

**Accessibility**

- Labels sit above inputs and stay visible; placeholder text is not a label.
- Errors are tied with aria-describedby and announced through a live region; the submit control is disabled only with an explanation.
- Group related fields with fieldset and legend — dates, addresses and consent especially.

**Performance**

- Chat, WhatsApp and exit-intent scripts load after interaction; they are the usual cause of a failing LCP and TBT.
- Submit with fetch and render inline state instead of navigating away from the page.

**Quality gate**

- Submit every form empty, invalid and valid, and confirm three visibly distinct states.
- The success state names what happens next and carries a reference the visitor can quote.
- Verify the enquiry actually reached its destination before launch, not just that the button turned green.

```jsx
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>

        </div>

          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
```

#### ContactForm

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/forms/ContactForm.jsx`
- **Core features:** Name · email · phone · message · consent · attachment
- **Purpose:** The baseline enquiry path every business needs, working on the worst connection.
- **Rule:** Five fields or fewer. Every extra field costs completions; attachments need a size limit and a type allowlist.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “ContactForm — five fields, three visible states”
- **Note:** One shared schema on both sides; errors tied to their field; the success screen carries a reference.

```jsx
const schema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(2, "Tell us your name"),
  email: z.string().email("Use a valid email address"),
  phone: z.string().optional(),
  message: z.string().min(12, "A sentence or two is enough"),
  consent: z.literal(true, { errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please agree before sending" }) }),
});

function ContactForm() {
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(schema),
    mode: "onBlur",
  });
  const send = useServerFn(submitEnquiry);
  const [reference, setReference] = useState(null);

  if (reference) {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-primary/40 bg-card p-8">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-2xl">Thank you — your enquiry is in.</h2>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          We reply within one working day. Your reference is
          <span className="ml-1 font-mono text-foreground">{reference}</span>.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      onSubmit={handleSubmit(async (values) => {
        const result = await send({ data: values });
        setReference(result.reference);
      })}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      {["name", "email", "message"].map((field) => (
        <div key={field}>
          <label htmlFor={field} className="mb-2 block text-sm">
            {labels[field]}
          </label>
          <input
            id={field}
            {...register(field)}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(formState.errors[field])}
            aria-describedby={formState.errors[field] ? field + "-error" : undefined}
            className="w-full border border-input bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-sm
                       focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
          />
          {formState.errors[field] && (
            <p id={field + "-error"} role="alert" className="mt-2 text-xs text-destructive">
              {formState.errors[field].message}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}

      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <input id="consent" type="checkbox" {...register("consent")} className="mt-1" />
        <label htmlFor="consent" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          I agree to be contacted about my enquiry. See the privacy notice.
        </label>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={formState.isSubmitting}
        className="rounded-full bg-primary px-7 py-3 text-sm font-medium
                   text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {formState.isSubmitting ? "Sending…" : "Send enquiry"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### CallNowPanel

```jsx
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">CallNowPanel</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### NewsletterSignup

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/conversion/NewsletterSignup.jsx`
- **Core features:** Value promise · frequency · privacy link
- **Purpose:** A small, low-commitment capture that compounds.
- **Rule:** State the cadence and the promise; double opt-in where the jurisdiction requires it.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// NewsletterSignup — conversion
// A small, low-commitment capture that compounds.
// Rule: State the cadence and the promise; double opt-in where the jurisdiction requires it.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Value promise · frequency · privacy link
export default function NewsletterSignup({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### Conversion modules — Growth tier (14)

#### BookingCalendar

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/booking/BookingCalendar.jsx`
- **Core features:** Service · location · staff · date · time · confirmation
- **Purpose:** Self-service scheduling that removes the phone call entirely.
- **Rule:** Compute slots server-side in the venue's timezone and re-check availability at confirmation.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “BookingCalendar — date strip, slot grid, named timezone”
- **Note:** Slots come from the server; the component only renders what it was given and re-checks the chosen slot on confirm.

```jsx
export function BookingCalendar({ service, days, slots, timezone, onConfirm }) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(days[0]);
  const [slot, setSlot] = useState(null);

  return (
    <div>
      <fieldset>
        <legend className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
          Choose a day
        </legend>
        <div role="group" className="mt-3 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
          {days.map((day) => (
            <button
              key={day.iso}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={date.iso === day.iso}
              onClick={() => { setDate(day); setSlot(null); }}
              className={
                "shrink-0 border px-4 py-3 text-center " +
                (date.iso === day.iso ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border")
              }
            >
              <span className="block font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
                {day.weekday}
              </span>
              <span className="mt-1 block text-lg tabular-nums">{day.dayOfMonth}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="mt-8">
        <legend className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
          Available times ({timezone})
        </legend>
        {slots.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            No times left on this day. Try another date, or call us on 0161 000 0000.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div role="group" className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5">
            {slots.map((option) => (
              <button
                key={option.iso}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={slot === option.iso}
                onClick={() => setSlot(option.iso)}
                className={
                  "border py-2.5 text-sm tabular-nums " +
                  (slot === option.iso ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border")
                }
              >
                {option.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </fieldset>

      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={!slot}
        onClick={() => onConfirm({ serviceId: service.id, startsAt: slot, timezone })}
        className="mt-8 w-full rounded-full bg-primary py-3 text-sm font-medium
                   text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {slot ? "Confirm booking" : "Choose a time to continue"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```
