#### CartDrawer

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/conversion/CartDrawer.jsx`
- **Core features:** Items · quantity · subtotal · promo code · checkout
- **Purpose:** Keeps the shopper in context while the basket stays visible.
- **Rule:** Recompute totals server-side on every mutation; a promo code validated only in the browser is a discount for everyone.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// CartDrawer — conversion
// Keeps the shopper in context while the basket stays visible.
// Rule: Recompute totals server-side on every mutation; a promo code validated only in the browser is a discount for everyone.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Items · quantity · subtotal · promo code · checkout
export default function CartDrawer({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### WaitlistForm

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/conversion/WaitlistForm.jsx`
- **Core features:** Capacity · priority · notification consent
- **Purpose:** Demand capture when supply is genuinely limited.
- **Rule:** State the position or the expectation honestly; a fake queue position is a support ticket.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// WaitlistForm — conversion
// Demand capture when supply is genuinely limited.
// Rule: State the position or the expectation honestly; a fake queue position is a support ticket.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Capacity · priority · notification consent
export default function WaitlistForm({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

### Conversion modules — Business tier (12)

#### MultiStepIntake

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/forms/MultiStepIntake.jsx`
- **Core features:** Progress bar · conditional fields · validation · save draft
- **Purpose:** Long intake broken into short, completable steps.
- **Rule:** Validate per step, keep the back button working, and never lose answers on refresh.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “MultiStepIntake — per-step validation, saved draft”
- **Note:** Step count in text, back never loses answers, and the draft key namespaces by form version.

```jsx
const steps = [
  { id: "need", fields: ["need", "urgency"] },
  { id: "detail", fields: ["detail", "budget"] },
  { id: "contact", fields: ["name", "email", "consent"] },
];

function MultiStepIntake({ onComplete }) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [values, setValues] = useState(() => readDraft() || {});

  useEffect(() => writeDraft(values), [values]);

  const step = steps[index];
  const isLast = index === steps.length - 1;

  const next = async () => {
    const parsed = stepSchema(step).safeParse(values);
    if (!parsed.success) return setError(parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors);
    setError({});
    if (isLast) return onComplete(values);
    setIndex(index + 1);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-4">
        <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-border">
          <div
            className="h-px bg-primary transition-all duration-500"
            style={{ width: ((index + 1) / steps.length) * 100 + "%" }}
          />
        </div>
        <p className="font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          Step {index + 1} of {steps.length}
        </p>
      </div>

      <p aria-live="polite" className="sr-only">{step.id} step</p>
      <StepFields step={step} values={values} onChange={setValues} errors={error} />

      <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIndex(Math.max(0, index - 1))}
          disabled={index === 0}
          className="text-sm text-muted-foreground disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          Back
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={next} className="rounded-full bg-primary px-7 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground">
          {isLast ? "Send enquiry" : "Continue"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### ReferralPanel

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/conversion/ReferralPanel.jsx`

```jsx
// ReferralPanel — conversion
// Turns satisfied customers into a channel.
// Rule: Explain the reward precisely, and only contact referred people with a lawful basis.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Referrer · friend contact · reward explanation
export default function ReferralPanel({ rows = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing here yet" }) {
  if (!rows.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">ReferralPanel</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
```

#### RFQForm

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/conversion/RFQForm.jsx`
- **Core features:** Specifications · files · quantity · deadline
- **Purpose:** Detailed buyer requirements for manufacturing, wholesale and B2B services.
- **Rule:** Allow file upload with progress, and confirm receipt with a reference number and a named contact.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// RFQForm — conversion
// Detailed buyer requirements for manufacturing, wholesale and B2B services.
// Rule: Allow file upload with progress, and confirm receipt with a reference number and a named contact.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Specifications · files · quantity · deadline
export default function RFQForm({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### ValuationRequest

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/conversion/ValuationRequest.jsx`
- **Core features:** Property details · photos · contact · appointment
- **Purpose:** Converts an owner's curiosity into a booked valuation.
- **Rule:** Progressive disclosure — address first, then details — with photos optional at the start.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ValuationRequest — conversion
// Converts an owner's curiosity into a booked valuation.
// Rule: Progressive disclosure — address first, then details — with photos optional at the start.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Property details · photos · contact · appointment
export default function ValuationRequest({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

---

## 06 · Commerce modules

Catalogue, basket and checkout — the parts that must never be wrong about price or stock.

**Architecture**

- Prices, tax and stock come from the server on every render. The client may display a total; it never decides one.
- The cart is a server-side record keyed to a session or account, mirrored in local state for instant feedback.
- Payment is verified server-side before an order is marked paid or access is granted — a success redirect is not proof.

**Accessibility**

- Cart and variant changes announce through a live region; a silently updated subtotal is invisible to a screen reader.
- Filter panels are dialogs on mobile with focus trapping, and inline controls on desktop.

**Performance**

- Paginate the grid rather than loading the catalogue; images get explicit dimensions and responsive sources.
- Keep the quick-view payload small — it is a preview, not the detail page duplicated.

**Quality gate**

- Test the basket with an out-of-stock item, a price that changed mid-session, and a failed payment.
- Compare the server total against the client total on every basket mutation.

### Commerce modules — Starter tier (5)

#### ProductGrid

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `routes/products.index.jsx`
- **Core features:** Filters · sort · pagination · quick view
- **Purpose:** The browse surface: filter, sort, page, preview without leaving.
- **Rule:** Run filters server-side and keep them in the URL so a filtered view can be shared and indexed.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “ProductGrid — filters live in the URL”
- **Note:** Server-side filtering and pagination; the grid reads query params so a filtered view is shareable.

```jsx
export const Route = createFileRoute("/products/")({
  validateSearch: (search) => ({
    category: search.category ?? undefined,
    sort: search.sort ?? undefined,
    page: Number(search.page ?? 1),
  }),
  loaderDeps: ({ search }) => search,
  loader: ({ deps }) => listProducts({ data: deps }),
  component: ProductsPage,
});

function ProductsPage() {
  const { items, next_cursor, has_more } = Route.useLoaderData();
  const search = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const setFilter = (patch) =>
    navigate({ to: "/products", search: (prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: 1 }) });

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-10 px-5 py-12 lg:grid-cols-[16rem_1fr] lg:px-10">
      <aside aria-label="Filters" className="space-y-8">
        {categories.map((category) => (
          <label key={category} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={search.category === category}
              onChange={() => setFilter({ category })}
            />
            {category}
          </label>
        ))}
      </aside>

      <section aria-live="polite">
        <p className="mb-6 font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          {items.length} products
        </p>
        <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {items.map((product) => <ProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />)}
        </div>
        {has_more && (
          <button onClick={() => setFilter({ cursor: next_cursor })} className="mt-10 rounded-full border border-border px-6 py-3 text-sm">
            Load more
          </button>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### VariantSelector

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/commerce/VariantSelector.jsx`
- **Core features:** Size · color · material · configuration
- **Purpose:** Chooses the exact SKU, with price and availability following the choice.
- **Rule:** Mark unavailable combinations before the click, not after; keep the chosen variant in the URL.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// VariantSelector — commerce
// Chooses the exact SKU, with price and availability following the choice.
// Rule: Mark unavailable combinations before the click, not after; keep the chosen variant in the URL.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Size · color · material · configuration
export default function VariantSelector({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
// Holds intent across sessions.
// Rule: Anonymous wishlists need a merge path on sign-in; otherwise the saved item disappears.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Save · share · move to cart
export default function Wishlist({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}
            <p className="font-heading text-base">{item.title}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

#### CompareDrawer

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/commerce/CompareDrawer.jsx`
- **Core features:** Side-by-side specifications
- **Purpose:** Narrows a shortlist on the attributes that differ.
- **Rule:** Compare three or four items maximum, aligned rows, differences emphasised.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// CompareDrawer — commerce
// Narrows a shortlist on the attributes that differ.
// Rule: Compare three or four items maximum, aligned rows, differences emphasised.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Side-by-side specifications
export default function CompareDrawer({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### StoreLocator

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/commerce/StoreLocator.jsx`
- **Core features:** Map · hours · services · directions · stock
- **Purpose:** Finds the nearest place that can actually serve the visitor.
- **Rule:** Load the map library lazily, offer a list view first, and never make the map the only way in.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// StoreLocator — commerce
// Finds the nearest place that can actually serve the visitor.
// Rule: Load the map library lazily, offer a list view first, and never make the map the only way in.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Map · hours · services · directions · stock
export default function StoreLocator({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### TaxShippingEstimator

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/commerce/TaxShippingEstimator.jsx`
- **Core features:** Address-based estimate
- **Purpose:** Removes the surprise that abandons checkouts.
- **Rule:** Estimate with an explicit 'before tax' caveat and recalculate at checkout from the same rules engine.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// TaxShippingEstimator — commerce
// Removes the surprise that abandons checkouts.
// Rule: Estimate with an explicit 'before tax' caveat and recalculate at checkout from the same rules engine.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Address-based estimate
export default function TaxShippingEstimator({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

---

## 07 · Booking, scheduling, and operations

Booking is a calendar problem before it is a design problem.

**Architecture**

- Store availability as rules — opening hours, blackout dates, capacity, resource assignments — and compute slots server-side in the venue's timezone.
- A slot is offerable only if the server confirms it is still free at the moment of write; re-check immediately before the insert.
- Confirmations carry an idempotency key so a double-tap cannot double-book, and a status the client can poll.

**Accessibility**

- The date/time grid is a real grid: arrow keys move by day and week, Enter selects, and the selected state is announced.
- Time zones appear in the label ('14:00 GMT+2'), never only in a tooltip.

**Performance**

- Fetch availability per service and date range, not the whole year.
- Cache the calendar shell and stream slots; a blocked screen reads as a broken booking.

**Quality gate**

- Book, reschedule and cancel the same appointment and verify each state on both the customer and staff sides.
- Test across a daylight-saving boundary and across two timezones.

### Booking, scheduling, and operations — Growth tier (5)

#### AvailabilityCalendar

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `lib/booking.js`
- **Core features:** Open slots · timezone · capacity · blackout dates
- **Purpose:** The core scheduling surface: what is genuinely free.
- **Rule:** Generate slots from rules on the server, and treat a blackout as a first-class record, not an empty day.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “AvailabilitySlots — computed on the server”
- **Note:** Rules in, slots out. Nothing about availability is decided in the browser.

```jsx
export function buildSlots({ date, hours, duration, buffer, bookings }) {
  const day = new Date(date).getDay();
  const window = hours[day];
  if (!window) return [];

  const slots = [];
  const step = (duration + buffer) * 60 * 1000;
  let cursor = toMinutes(window.open, date);
  const close = toMinutes(window.close, date);

  while (cursor + duration * 60000 <= close) {
    const end = cursor + duration * 60000;
    const clash = bookings.some((b) => cursor < b.end && end > b.start);
    if (!clash) slots.push({ start: new Date(cursor), end: new Date(end) });
    cursor += step;
  }

  return slots;
}

// The write path re-checks before insert, inside a transaction:
export async function confirmBooking(base44, { slot, serviceId, staffId }) {
  const taken = await base44.entities.Booking.filter({
    staff_id: staffId,
    starts_at: slot.start.toISOString(),
    status: { $ne: "cancelled" },
  });
  if (taken.length > 0) throw new Error("That slot has just been taken.");
  return base44.entities.Booking.create({ service_id: serviceId, ...slot });
}
```

#### StaffSelector

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/booking/StaffSelector.jsx`
- **Core features:** Team member · specialty · language · rating
- **Purpose:** Routes the booking to someone who can actually do it.
- **Rule:** Order by availability, not alphabetically, and allow 'any available' as a real option.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// StaffSelector — booking, scheduling, and operations
// Routes the booking to someone who can actually do it.
// Rule: Order by availability, not alphabetically, and allow 'any available' as a real option.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Team member · specialty · language · rating
export default function StaffSelector({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### PatientIntake

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/booking/PatientIntake.jsx`
- **Core features:** Medical history · consent · insurance · e-signature
- **Purpose:** Clinical history captured before the appointment, once.
- **Rule:** Treat this as sensitive data: private storage, least-privilege access, audit trail, explicit consent per purpose.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PatientIntake — booking, scheduling, and operations
// Clinical history captured before the appointment, once.
// Rule: Treat this as sensitive data: private storage, least-privilege access, audit trail, explicit consent per purpose.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Medical history · consent · insurance · e-signature
export default function PatientIntake({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
// QueueStatus — booking, scheduling, and operations
// Manages the wait instead of leaving people guessing.

            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>

              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
```

---

## 08 · Location and local SEO modules

Local businesses should show consistent name, address, phone, hours, map, and booking/contact paths.

**Architecture**

- One location record feeds every surface: header, footer, cards, map and structured data. A phone number typed twice will eventually disagree with itself.
- Store hours as structured data — day, opens, closes, date exceptions — and derive open/closed from it instead of writing 'open now' by hand.
- Every location deserves a stable URL. Neighbourhood and service-area pages must carry unique content, or they will be treated as duplicates.

**Accessibility**

- Maps are decoration unless they are the way to find the place: always provide an address and directions link in text.
- Open/closed state is text plus colour, never a green dot alone.

**Performance**

- Map libraries are heavy — load on interaction or on scroll into view, and reserve the container height.
- Address and hours are server-rendered so they exist without JavaScript.

**Quality gate**

- Cross-check name, address and phone against the Google Business Profile character for character.
- Test hours rendering on a public holiday exception and across the timezone boundary.

#### GoogleMapEmbed

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/local/GoogleMapEmbed.jsx`
- **Core features:** Map · directions · accessibility · loading state
- **Purpose:** Confirms the place is real and where the entrance is.
- **Rule:** Lazy-load the embed, keep a text address beside it, and never trap keyboard users inside the iframe.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// GoogleMapEmbed — location and local seo
// Confirms the place is real and where the entrance is.
// Rule: Lazy-load the embed, keep a text address beside it, and never trap keyboard users inside the iframe.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Map · directions · accessibility · loading state
export default function GoogleMapEmbed({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### OpenNowBadge

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/local/OpenNowBadge.jsx`
- **Core features:** Live open/closed state
- **Purpose:** Answers the immediate question before the phone call.
- **Rule:** Compute from the hours record on the server, and show the next opening time when closed.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// OpenNowBadge — location and local seo
// Answers the immediate question before the phone call.
// Rule: Compute from the hours record on the server, and show the next opening time when closed.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Live open/closed state
export default function OpenNowBadge({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Location and local SEO modules — Growth tier (4)

#### ServiceAreaMap

- **Purpose:** Defines where the business will actually travel to.
- **Rule:** State exclusions explicitly — a zone map without them generates out-of-area enquiries.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ServiceAreaMap — location and local seo
// Defines where the business will actually travel to.
// Rule: State exclusions explicitly — a zone map without them generates out-of-area enquiries.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Coverage zones · postcode checker · exclusions
export default function ServiceAreaMap({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

---

## 09 · Content and SEO modules

Content modules carry most of the search weight; structure decides whether they get read.

**Architecture**

- One canonical URL per document, a real heading hierarchy, and a self-referencing canonical tag. Tags and filters should not create duplicates.
- Store article structure as data — headings, media, author, dates — so tables of contents, related content and JSON-LD are derived, not hand-maintained.
- Publish and modified dates plus author identity are first-class fields. They power Article structured data and the trust signals readers check.

**Accessibility**

- A table of contents is a navigation landmark of in-page links with a visible current-section state.
- Video and audio need captions and transcripts; downloads state their format and size before the click.

**Performance**

- Render articles as server-rendered HTML; never ship a client-side markdown parser for the main content.
- Paginate indexes and lazy-load below-the-fold media with fixed dimensions.

**Quality gate**

- Validate structured data before launch and re-validate after any template change.
- Check every page has one h1, a unique title, and a description that reads like a sentence.

### Content and SEO modules — Starter tier (2)

#### FAQAccordion

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/sections/FaqAccordion.jsx`
- **Core features:** Grouped questions · search · schema support
- **Purpose:** Answers the objections that block a decision, in the order they arise.
- **Rule:** Use native disclosure semantics, keep answers in the DOM, and emit FAQPage structured data only for visible questions.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “FAQAccordion — native disclosure, schema-ready”
- **Note:** Answers stay in the DOM. Structured data only covers questions visible on the page.

```jsx
export function FaqAccordion({ groups }) {
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-5 py-20">
      {groups.map((group) => (
        <div key={group.title} className="mb-12">
          <h2 className="font-mono text-[0.7rem] uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-primary">
            {group.title}
          </h2>
          <div className="mt-6 divide-y divide-border border-y border-border">
            {group.items.map((item, index) => (
              <details key={item.q} open={index === 0} className="group">
                <summary className="flex cursor-pointer list-none items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5">
                  <span className="text-base">{item.q}</span>
                  <PlusIcon
                    className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform group-open:rotate-45"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                </summary>
                <div className="pb-5 pr-10 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                  {item.a}
                </div>
              </details>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### StructuredDataBlock

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/seo/StructuredData.jsx`
- **Core features:** LocalBusiness · Service · Product · FAQ · Article · Event · JobPosting
- **Purpose:** Machine-readable facts that search engines and AI assistants reuse.
- **Rule:** Emit JSON-LD from the same data the page renders, and validate it after every template change.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “StructuredData — emitted from the same record”
- **Note:** One source for visible content and JSON-LD. Validate after every template change; never describe hidden or invented facts.

```jsx
export function LocalBusinessSchema({ location }) {
  const data = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "LocalBusiness",
    name: location.name,
    telephone: location.phone,
    address: {
      "@type": "PostalAddress",
      streetAddress: location.street,
      addressLocality: location.city,
      postalCode: location.postcode,
      addressCountry: location.country,
    },
    openingHoursSpecification: location.hours.map((slot) => ({
      "@type": "OpeningHoursSpecification",
      dayOfWeek: slot.day,
      opens: slot.opens,
      closes: slot.closes,
    })),
    url: location.url,
  };

  return (
    <script
      type="application/ld+json"
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(data) }}
    />
  );
}

// FAQPage only for questions rendered visibly on the page:
// { "@type": "Question", name: item.q, acceptedAnswer: { "@type": "Answer", text: item.a } }
```

#### ArticleTemplate

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/content/ArticleTemplate.jsx`
- **Core features:** Title · author · date · TOC · media · related posts
- **Purpose:** The reading surface: one column, one measure, no competing calls to action.
- **Rule:** Cap the measure near 70 characters, set the TOC from real headings, and keep the byline above the fold.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “ArticleTemplate — measure, byline, generated TOC”
- **Note:** The table of contents comes from real headings; the byline and dates are fields, not decoration.

```jsx
export function ArticleTemplate({ article, headings }) {
  return (
    <article className="mx-auto grid max-w-[80rem] gap-12 px-5 py-16 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_16rem] lg:px-10">
      <div>
        <header className="max-w-[68ch]">
          <p className="font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-primary">
            {article.category}
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-5 font-heading text-[clamp(2rem,4vw,3.25rem)] leading-[1.1] tracking-[-0.02em]">
            {article.title}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            <a href={"/authors/" + article.authorSlug} className="text-foreground underline underline-offset-4">
              {article.author}
            </a>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <time dateTime={article.published}>{formatDate(article.published)}</time>
            {article.updated && <span>Updated {formatDate(article.updated)}</span>}
          </p>
        </header>

        <div className="prose prose-invert mt-10 max-w-[68ch]"><PortableText value={article.body} /></div>
      </div>

      <aside className="hidden lg:block">
        <nav aria-label="On this page" className="sticky top-24">
          <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Contents</p>
          <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 border-l border-border">
            {headings.map((heading) => (
              <li key={heading.id}>
                <a href={"#" + heading.id} className="block border-l border-transparent py-1 pl-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary hover:text-foreground">
                  {heading.text}
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>
      </aside>
    </article>
  );
}
```

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/seo/StructuredData.jsx`
- **Core features:** Responsibilities · requirements · salary · apply
- **Purpose:** The role, written for the person deciding whether to apply.
- **Rule:** Publish the salary range and the process length; vague listings lose the strongest candidates.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “StructuredData — emitted from the same record”
- **Note:** One source for visible content and JSON-LD. Validate after every template change; never describe hidden or invented facts.

```jsx
export function LocalBusinessSchema({ location }) {
  const data = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "LocalBusiness",
    name: location.name,
    telephone: location.phone,
    address: {
      "@type": "PostalAddress",
      streetAddress: location.street,
      addressLocality: location.city,
      postalCode: location.postcode,
      addressCountry: location.country,
    },
    openingHoursSpecification: location.hours.map((slot) => ({
      "@type": "OpeningHoursSpecification",
      dayOfWeek: slot.day,
      opens: slot.opens,
      closes: slot.closes,
    })),
    url: location.url,
  };

  return (
    <script
      type="application/ld+json"
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(data) }}
    />
  );
}

// FAQPage only for questions rendered visibly on the page:
// { "@type": "Question", name: item.q, acceptedAnswer: { "@type": "Answer", text: item.a } }
```

```jsx
    <section className="border-b border-border">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-6xl gap-10 px-5 py-16 lg:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr] lg:px-10">
        <div>
          {eyebrow ? <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-primary">{eyebrow}</p> : null}
          <h2 className="mt-4 font-heading text-[clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)] leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em]">{heading}</h2>
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>

}
```

#### PodcastEpisodeList

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/content/PodcastEpisodeList.jsx`
- **Core features:** Episodes · guests · audio player · transcript
- **Purpose:** Episodes with the metadata that makes them findable.
- **Rule:** Ship a transcript per episode and an audio player with keyboard controls and a download link.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PodcastEpisodeList — content and seo
// Episodes with the metadata that makes them findable.
// Rule: Ship a transcript per episode and an audio player with keyboard controls and a download link.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Episodes · guests · audio player · transcript
export default function PodcastEpisodeList({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}
            <p className="font-heading text-base">{item.title}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

#### CaseStudyIndex

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/content/CaseStudyIndex.jsx`

- **Purpose:** Proof browsable by the buyer's own context.

```jsx
// CaseStudyIndex — content and seo
// Proof browsable by the buyer's own context.
// Rule: Filter by industry or result, and show the headline metric on each card.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Industry · service · result filters
export default function CaseStudyIndex({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

---

## 10 · People, team, and culture

People pages convert service businesses; they need credentials, not portraits alone.

**Architecture**

- One person record, rendered as a card, a profile and a booking link. Never duplicate a bio into three templates.
- Profiles are staff-facing URLs: they should survive a change of role, so slug on a stable id or a permanent name.
- Availability belongs to the person and their schedule, not to a paragraph of copy.

**Accessibility**

- Portraits carry the person's name as alt text, or are marked decorative when the name sits beside them.
- Booking links are real links with descriptive text — 'Book with Dr Chen', not 'Book'.

**Performance**

- Serve portraits in two sizes: a grid thumbnail and a profile hero. Never the hero image in the grid.
- Lazy-load the grid below the fold with fixed aspect ratios to avoid layout shift.

**Quality gate**

- Check every credential, registration number and language claim with the person before publishing.
- Confirm that removing a person from the team does not leave a broken profile link in articles.

### People, team, and culture — Starter tier (1)

#### TeamGrid

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/people/TeamGrid.jsx`
- **Core features:** Photo · name · role · specialties · booking
- **Purpose:** The whole team at a glance, with a route into each person.
- **Rule:** Consistent crop and aspect ratio; inconsistent portraits are the most visible craft failure on these pages.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// TeamGrid — people, team, and culture
// The whole team at a glance, with a route into each person.
// Rule: Consistent crop and aspect ratio; inconsistent portraits are the most visible craft failure on these pages.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Photo · name · role · specialties · booking
export default function TeamGrid({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}
            <p className="font-heading text-base">{item.title}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

#### CareersCulture

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/people/CareersCulture.jsx`
- **Core features:** Values · benefits · openings · employee stories
- **Purpose:** Shows what working here is actually like.
- **Rule:** Concrete benefits and named employee stories beat a values list nobody can verify.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// CareersCulture — people, team, and culture
// Shows what working here is actually like.
// Rule: Concrete benefits and named employee stories beat a values list nobody can verify.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Values · benefits · openings · employee stories
export default function CareersCulture({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

## 11 · Hospitality and food modules

Menus, rooms and reservations change weekly — model them so an operator can update without a developer.

**Architecture**

- Menu items, rooms and experiences are records with price, availability and dietary/allergen fields — not a PDF and not a paragraph.
- Availability drives the CTA: a room that cannot be booked should say so, and a menu item marked unavailable must leave the list.
- Prices need a currency, a tax treatment and an effective date. Hospitality pricing changes seasonally.

**Accessibility**

- Menus are structured lists with headings per course, not images of a printed card.
- Dietary markers need a legend in text, not colour alone.

**Performance**

- Optimise food and interior photography aggressively — it is usually the largest payload on these sites.
- Reservation widgets load lazily; the phone number must work without them.

**Quality gate**

- Ask the operator to update one price and one availability themselves before handover.
- Verify allergen information is present on every item that needs it.

#### MenuPDFDownload

#### HousePolicyPanel

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/hospitality/HousePolicyPanel.jsx`
- **Core features:** Check-in · cancellation · pets · accessibility
- **Purpose:** The rules, in the place people look for them.
- **Rule:** Use a definition list, and repeat the cancellation window on the booking confirmation.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// HousePolicyPanel — hospitality and food
// The rules, in the place people look for them.
// Rule: Use a definition list, and repeat the cancellation window on the booking confirmation.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Check-in · cancellation · pets · accessibility
export default function HousePolicyPanel({ rows = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing here yet" }) {
  if (!rows.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">HousePolicyPanel</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### RoomCard

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/hospitality/RoomCard.jsx`
- **Core features:** Photos · amenities · price · availability
- **Purpose:** One room type, with the price and whether it is free.
- **Rule:** Show the total for the stay, not the nightly rate alone.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// RoomCard — hospitality and food
// One room type, with the price and whether it is free.
// Rule: Show the total for the stay, not the nightly rate alone.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Photos · amenities · price · availability
export default function RoomCard({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### GroupBookingForm

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/hospitality/GroupBookingForm.jsx`
- **Core features:** Rooms · dates · event needs · contact
- **Purpose:** Multi-room bookings need a human, but a structured one.
- **Rule:** Break rooms into a quantity grid and capture the event requirement separately.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// GroupBookingForm — hospitality and food
// Multi-room bookings need a human, but a structured one.
// Rule: Break rooms into a quantity grid and capture the event requirement separately.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Rooms · dates · event needs · contact
export default function GroupBookingForm({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### LoyaltySignup

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/hospitality/LoyaltySignup.jsx`
- **Core features:** Member rates · perks · account creation
- **Purpose:** The reason a guest books direct next time.
- **Rule:** Name the concrete perks and the member rate; 'exclusive benefits' converts nobody.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// LoyaltySignup — hospitality and food
// The reason a guest books direct next time.
// Rule: Name the concrete perks and the member rate; 'exclusive benefits' converts nobody.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Member rates · perks · account creation
export default function LoyaltySignup({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

---

## 12 · Real estate and property modules

Listings live or die on filter fidelity, map sync and how fast a photo grid loads.

**Architecture**

- A listing is a record with typed fields — beds, baths, area, status, price — never free text. Filters can only be honest if the data is.
- URL is the state: filters, map viewport and sort belong in query parameters so a search can be shared, reloaded and indexed.
- Status is the source of truth for the CTA. Sold, under offer, withdrawn and off-market change the whole page.

**Accessibility**

- The map is never the only route to a listing; the list is primary and the map is an enhancement.
- Price ranges and property attributes are readable in text, with units spelled out.

**Performance**

- Image weight is the entire performance story: responsive sources, modern formats, and a fixed aspect ratio per card.
- Filter with server-side queries and paginate; loading 2,000 listings to filter in the browser is the classic failure.

**Quality gate**

- Verify a saved search produces the same results as the live filter after a reload.
- Check a listing with missing fields — no photo, no floorplan — renders a sensible card.

### Real estate and property modules — Growth tier (6)

#### ListingGrid

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/property/ListingGrid.jsx`
- **Core features:** Filters · map sync · sort · saved search
- **Purpose:** The search results surface, where most of the traffic actually lands.
- **Rule:** Filters server-side, state in the URL, and a result count that updates as filters change.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ListingGrid — real estate and property
// The search results surface, where most of the traffic actually lands.
// Rule: Filters server-side, state in the URL, and a result count that updates as filters change.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Filters · map sync · sort · saved search
export default function ListingGrid({ items = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing to show yet" }) {
  if (!items.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
          <a href={item.href} className="group block">
            {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" loading="lazy" className="mb-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover" /> : null}
            <p className="font-heading text-base">{item.title}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.summary}</p>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

#### ListingCard

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/property/ListingCard.jsx`
- **Core features:** Photo · price · beds · baths · area · status
- **Purpose:** One property, scannable and comparable with its neighbours.
- **Rule:** Same field order on every card, price as the most prominent element, status as a badge.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ListingCard — real estate and property
// One property, scannable and comparable with its neighbours.
// Rule: Same field order on every card, price as the most prominent element, status as a badge.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Photo · price · beds · baths · area · status
export default function ListingCard({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

---

## 13 · Automotive modules

Vehicle stock changes hourly; the site must reflect that or it becomes a brochure.

**Architecture**

- A vehicle is a record keyed by registration or VIN, with stock status, mileage and price history.
- Finance figures are computed from the same rate table the dealership uses, with regulated disclosures attached.
- Service booking and vehicle sales are separate funnels — do not merge them into one form.

**Accessibility**

- Specification tables use proper headers and consistent units (miles vs km) per market.
- Galleries need keyboard controls and descriptive alt text naming the angle.

**Performance**

- Vehicle photography is the payload problem: consistent crops, responsive sizes, lazy loading.
- Filter server-side; stock changes mean cached pages must be short-lived or revalidated.

**Quality gate**

- Check a sold vehicle is removed from search results, not just marked on its detail page.
- Verify finance examples include the representative APR and the total payable.
