#### VehicleDetail

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/automotive/VehicleDetail.jsx`
- **Core features:** Gallery · specs · history · finance · test drive
- **Purpose:** The full vehicle case, including the history that builds trust.
- **Rule:** Service history, previous owners and MOT/roadworthiness status belong on this page, not on request.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// VehicleDetail — automotive
// The full vehicle case, including the history that builds trust.
// Rule: Service history, previous owners and MOT/roadworthiness status belong on this page, not on request.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Gallery · specs · history · finance · test drive
export default function VehicleDetail({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### FinanceCalculator

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/automotive/FinanceCalculator.jsx`
- **Core features:** Vehicle price · deposit · term · APR
- **Purpose:** Monthly figures with the assumptions visible.
- **Rule:** Regulated markets require the representative example and a clear 'not a credit offer' line.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// FinanceCalculator — automotive
// Monthly figures with the assumptions visible.
// Rule: Regulated markets require the representative example and a clear 'not a credit offer' line.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Vehicle price · deposit · term · APR
export default function FinanceCalculator({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### PartsCatalogue

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/automotive/PartsCatalogue.jsx`
- **Core features:** Compatibility · SKU · stock · enquiry
- **Purpose:** Parts matched to vehicles, not browsed blind.
- **Rule:** Vehicle compatibility is the primary filter; a part that 'may fit' generates returns.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PartsCatalogue — automotive
// Parts matched to vehicles, not browsed blind.
// Rule: Vehicle compatibility is the primary filter; a part that 'may fit' generates returns.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Compatibility · SKU · stock · enquiry
export default function PartsCatalogue({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

---

## 14 · Education and learning modules

Learners need to see the path, the commitment and the proof before they enrol.

**Architecture**

- Model the hierarchy explicitly: programme ? course ? module ? lesson. Progress belongs to the enrolment record, not the course.
- Access is granted server-side after payment or approval, and checked on every lesson request — not hidden in the client.
- Certificates reference a verifiable id so an employer can check them.

**Accessibility**

- Lesson video needs captions and a transcript; quizzes need keyboard-operable options and text feedback.
- Progress is stated as text ('4 of 12 lessons'), not a bar alone.

**Performance**

- Stream video, never self-host large files; lazy-load the player until the lesson opens.
- Course indexes are server-rendered and paginated so they are discoverable.

**Quality gate**

- Complete a lesson, a quiz and a certificate as a real enrolled learner end to end.
- Verify a logged-out visitor cannot open a lesson URL directly.

#### CurriculumAccordion

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/education/CurriculumAccordion.jsx`
- **Core features:** Modules · lessons · duration · previews
- **Purpose:** Shows the structure and the commitment.
- **Rule:** Free previews on the first lesson of each module, and total duration in the summary row.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// CurriculumAccordion — education and learning
// Shows the structure and the commitment.
// Rule: Free previews on the first lesson of each module, and total duration in the summary row.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Modules · lessons · duration · previews
export default function CurriculumAccordion({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Education and learning modules — Business tier (8)

#### LessonPlayer

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/education/LessonPlayer.jsx`
- **Core features:** Video · transcript · notes · completion
- **Purpose:** The learning surface itself.
- **Rule:** Persist position, offer a transcript, and mark completion on a real event rather than page load.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// LessonPlayer — education and learning
// The learning surface itself.
// Rule: Persist position, offer a transcript, and mark completion on a real event rather than page load.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Video · transcript · notes · completion
export default function LessonPlayer({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### StudentDashboard

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/education/StudentDashboard.jsx`
- **Core features:** Courses · progress · assignments · messages
- **Purpose:** The learner's home after enrolment.
- **Rule:** Resume where they left off as the primary action; design the empty state for a new enrolment.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// StudentDashboard — education and learning
// The learner's home after enrolment.
// Rule: Resume where they left off as the primary action; design the empty state for a new enrolment.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Courses · progress · assignments · messages
export default function StudentDashboard({ rows = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing here yet" }) {
  if (!rows.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">StudentDashboard</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- **Rule:** Status per applicant, with the next required action highlighted.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// AdmissionsTimeline — education and learning
// Turns a complicated application into dated steps.
// Rule: Status per applicant, with the next required action highlighted.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Requirements · deadlines · status
export default function AdmissionsTimeline({ rows = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing here yet" }) {
  if (!rows.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">AdmissionsTimeline</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

## 15 · Events, venues, and entertainment

Events are date-bound, capacity-bound and timezone-bound — all three must be modelled.

**Architecture**

- An event has a timezone, a start, an end and a status. Store times in UTC and render in the event's own timezone with a relative hint.
- Ticket inventory is server-side and decremented atomically; seat holds expire.
- Past events remain at their URL with a clear 'this event has ended' state — never a 404 that breaks inbound links.

**Accessibility**

- Seat maps need a text alternative: a list of sections with availability is the accessible equivalent, not an afterthought.
- Agenda and schedule tables must be readable linearly, with times and titles in text.

**Performance**

- Timetables can be large — render server-side and paginate by day.
- Seat maps are canvas or SVG; keep the initial payload light and disable animation under reduced motion.

**Quality gate**

- Book a ticket, receive the confirmation, and cancel it within the policy window.
- Check the event page after the end date and confirm the ended state plus archive links.

#### EventDetail

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/events/EventDetail.jsx`
- **Core features:** Schedule · speakers · tickets · map · FAQ
- **Purpose:** Everything a ticket buyer needs before paying.
- **Rule:** Include the run time, the doors time, the venue address and the refund policy above the ticket selector.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// EventDetail — events, venues, and entertainment
// Everything a ticket buyer needs before paying.
// Rule: Include the run time, the doors time, the venue address and the refund policy above the ticket selector.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Schedule · speakers · tickets · map · FAQ
export default function EventDetail({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

### Events, venues, and entertainment — Business tier (8)

#### TicketSelector

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/events/TicketSelector.jsx`
- **Core features:** Tier · quantity · seat map · checkout
- **Purpose:** Tiers and quantities, moving into checkout.
- **Rule:** Cap quantities per order, show fees before the final step, and reserve inventory during checkout.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// TicketSelector — events, venues, and entertainment
// Tiers and quantities, moving into checkout.
// Rule: Cap quantities per order, show fees before the final step, and reserve inventory during checkout.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Tier · quantity · seat map · checkout
export default function TicketSelector({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### VenueSpaceCard

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/events/VenueSpaceCard.jsx`
- **Core features:** Capacity · layout · amenities · enquiry
- **Purpose:** One hireable space with its constraints.
- **Rule:** Capacity per layout (theatre, cabaret, standing) — one number is always wrong.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// VenueSpaceCard — events, venues, and entertainment
// One hireable space with its constraints.
// Rule: Capacity per layout (theatre, cabaret, standing) — one number is always wrong.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Capacity · layout · amenities · enquiry
export default function VenueSpaceCard({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### AgendaTimeline

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/events/AgendaTimeline.jsx`
- **Core features:** Multi-track schedule · personal agenda
- **Purpose:** The programme, across parallel tracks.
- **Rule:** Two-dimensional time and track on desktop, a linear list on mobile, and a conflict warning when saving.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// AgendaTimeline — events, venues, and entertainment
// The programme, across parallel tracks.
// Rule: Two-dimensional time and track on desktop, a linear list on mobile, and a conflict warning when saving.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Multi-track schedule · personal agenda
export default function AgendaTimeline({ rows = [], emptyLabel = "Nothing here yet" }) {
  if (!rows.length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{emptyLabel}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-border">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">AgendaTimeline</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Reference</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-3 text-right font-medium">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-border/60 last:border-b-0">
              <td className="p-3">{row.label}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-muted-foreground">{row.status}</td>
              <td className="p-3 text-right"><a href={row.href} className="text-primary hover:underline">Open</a></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### PressKitDownload

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/events/PressKitDownload.jsx`
- **Core features:** Bio · images · technical rider · contacts
- **Purpose:** What a promoter or journalist needs in one archive.
- **Rule:** One zip plus individual files, with a named contact and image credits.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PressKitDownload — events, venues, and entertainment
// What a promoter or journalist needs in one archive.
// Rule: One zip plus individual files, with a named contact and image credits.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Bio · images · technical rider · contacts
export default function PressKitDownload({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

---

## 16 · B2B, SaaS, and enterprise modules

Strong SaaS architecture usually centers on product, solutions/use cases, pricing, resources, about, contact, demo, and login.

**Architecture**

- Three audiences, three paths: evaluator (product and proof), buyer (pricing, security, procurement), user (login and docs). Route each one deliberately.
- Security and compliance pages are conversion pages, not legal footnotes — procurement will read them before anything else.
- Keep pricing logic in one place: plan limits, usage rates and the calculator must all read the same table.

**Accessibility**

- Code samples need a copy control with a keyboard path and readable contrast for syntax colours.
- Status and uptime indicators are text plus colour, with a plain-language summary.

**Performance**

- Docs sites carry the largest page counts: static-render everything and keep search client-side over a small index.
- Third-party status widgets and chat load after interaction.

**Quality gate**

- Read the pricing page as a buyer with a specific team size and confirm the total is unambiguous.
- Verify the demo form reaches a human and the calendar invite arrives.

#### PricingCalculator

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/b2b/PricingCalculator.jsx`
- **Core features:** Users · usage · plan · estimate
- **Purpose:** Makes usage-based pricing predictable.
- **Rule:** Show the crossing point where the next plan is cheaper — that honesty closes deals.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PricingCalculator — b2b, saas, and enterprise
// Makes usage-based pricing predictable.
// Rule: Show the crossing point where the next plan is cheaper — that honesty closes deals.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Users · usage · plan · estimate
export default function PricingCalculator({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### ProductChangelog

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/b2b/ProductChangelog.jsx`
- **Core features:** Release notes tied to roadmap
- **Purpose:** Shows momentum and direction.
- **Rule:** Separate shipped from planned, and date the roadmap rather than implying commitment.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ProductChangelog — b2b, saas, and enterprise
// Shows momentum and direction.
// Rule: Separate shipped from planned, and date the roadmap rather than implying commitment.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Release notes tied to roadmap
export default function ProductChangelog({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

### B2B, SaaS, and enterprise modules — Flagship tier (3)

#### ROIcalculator

- **Type:** Flagship
- **File:** `components/b2b/ROIcalculator.jsx`
- **Core features:** Inputs · assumptions · savings estimate
- **Purpose:** Turns a benefit claim into the buyer's own numbers.
- **Rule:** Publish the assumptions and let the buyer download the result to defend internally.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ROIcalculator — b2b, saas, and enterprise
// Turns a benefit claim into the buyer's own numbers.
// Rule: Publish the assumptions and let the buyer download the result to defend internally.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Inputs · assumptions · savings estimate
export default function ROIcalculator({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

---

## 17 · Finance, legal, and regulated modules

In regulated sectors the design job is disclosure: earn trust without overpromising in writing.

**Architecture**

- Sensitive documents live in private storage and are served through short-lived signed links with an audit trail.
- Every figure that could be read as advice needs its assumption, its date and its disclaimer rendered with it — not in a linked PDF.
- Portal access is authorised server-side per matter or account on every request; never merely hidden in the interface.

**Accessibility**

- Legal text needs the same contrast and size as body copy, not grey 11px as a legal fig leaf.
- Multi-step forms announce step changes and preserve entered values when moving backwards.

**Performance**

- Document upload and viewing are the heavy paths: chunked uploads, progress, and streamed previews.
- Calculators run locally; no network round trip per keystroke.

**Quality gate**

- Have the required disclosures reviewed by whoever owns compliance before launch.
- Test the portal as a second, unrelated user and confirm they cannot open the first user's documents.

```jsx
// CaseEvaluationForm — finance, legal, and regulated
// Qualifies a matter before it consumes a lawyer's time.
// Rule: Ask the date and the issue first, documents last; state the response time and that no advice is given yet.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Issue · date · documents · urgency
export default function CaseEvaluationForm({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>

  );
}
```

---

## 18 · Nonprofit and public-sector modules

Mission, impact and money must be equally legible — donors and auditors read the same site.

**Architecture**

- Impact claims are records with a metric, a period and a method. Derive the headlines from them rather than typing numbers into copy.
- Donation amounts, designations and frequencies are configuration, so finance can change them without a deploy.
- Public-sector content needs an owner and a review date per page; stale notices are a governance failure.

**Accessibility**

- Meet WCAG 2.2 AA as a baseline — public funding frequently requires a published accessibility statement.
- Forms asking for personal circumstances must explain why each field is needed.

**Performance**

- Campaign traffic is spiky: keep donation pages server-rendered, light, and functional without third-party scripts where possible.
- Annual reports are large PDFs — offer an HTML summary alongside.

**Quality gate**

- Make a real test donation and confirm the receipt and the acknowledgement email.
- Check the progress total recalculates from actual donations, not a hardcoded figure.

#### VolunteerSignup

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/nonprofit/VolunteerSignup.jsx`
- **Core features:** Role · availability · skills · onboarding
- **Purpose:** Matching people to roles they can actually do.
- **Rule:** Publish time commitments and any checks required (references, background checks) before signup.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// VolunteerSignup — nonprofit and public-sector
// Matching people to roles they can actually do.
// Rule: Publish time commitments and any checks required (references, background checks) before signup.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Role · availability · skills · onboarding
export default function VolunteerSignup({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```jsx
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### ServiceDirectory

- **Type:** Business
- **File:** `components/nonprofit/ServiceDirectory.jsx`
- **Core features:** Departments · contacts · hours · forms
- **Purpose:** Who to contact about what.
- **Rule:** One entry per service with a phone number and a form, not a switchboard number alone.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ServiceDirectory — nonprofit and public-sector
// Who to contact about what.
// Rule: One entry per service with a phone number and a form, not a switchboard number alone.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Departments · contacts · hours · forms
export default function ServiceDirectory({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

---

## 19 · Motion, interaction, and premium layers

Use these only after the functional site is excellent. Motion should support hierarchy rather than obscure content.

**Architecture**

- Motion is a layer, not a structure: every module must be correct with animation disabled and with JavaScript unavailable.
- Two motion systems maximum — one for entrances, one for interaction feedback — with shared easing and duration tokens.
- One signature moment per page. Three competing reveals read as a template, not a brand.

**Accessibility**

- Honour prefers-reduced-motion: replace movement with a short opacity change, never remove the content.
- Anything pointer-only (tilt, magnetic, cursor labels, drag) needs a keyboard and touch equivalent.
- Modals and lightboxes trap focus, close on Escape, and restore focus to the trigger.

**Performance**

- Animate transform and opacity only; animating layout properties causes the jank people associate with 'heavy' sites.
- 3D, WebGL and video load on interaction or idle, behind a poster, and after consent where relevant.

**Quality gate**

- Test with Reduced Motion enabled at OS level and confirm nothing breaks or disappears.
- Throttle to a mid-range phone profile and check the signature moment still holds 60fps.

### Motion, interaction, and premium layers — Growth tier (9)

#### RevealOnScroll

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/motion/RevealOnScroll.jsx`
- **Core features:** Fade/translate · IntersectionObserver · reduced-motion branch
- **Purpose:** Brings content in as it enters the viewport, if the page needs the rhythm at all.
- **Rule:** Never reveal above-the-fold content this way, and never leave content hidden if the observer fails.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “RevealOnScroll — content is never hidden permanently”
- **Note:** Above-the-fold content renders visible. Reduced motion shortens the transition instead of removing content.

```jsx
export function RevealOnScroll({ children, delay = 0, className }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    const node = ref.current;
    if (!node || reduced) return setVisible(true);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => entry.isIntersecting && setVisible(true),
      { rootMargin: "0px 0px -12% 0px", threshold: 0.15 },
    );
    observer.observe(node);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [reduced]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "transition-[opacity,transform] duration-700 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]",
        visible ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "translate-y-4 opacity-0",
        reduced && "translate-y-0",
        className,
      )}
      style={{ transitionDelay: reduced ? "0ms" : delay + "ms" }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### AnimatedCounter

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/motion/AnimatedCounter.jsx`
- **Core features:** Metrics count up when visible
- **Purpose:** Draws attention to a number that matters.
- **Rule:** Render the final value server-side and animate only on the client, so the number is never missing.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “AnimatedCounter — the number exists without JS”
- **Note:** Render the final value on the server, then animate from zero once visible and only if motion is allowed.

```jsx
export function AnimatedCounter({ value, suffix = "", label }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(value);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduced) return;
    setShown(0);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
      const start = performance.now();
      const tick = (now) => {
        const progress = Math.min(1, (now - start) / 900);
        setShown(Math.round(value * (1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3))));
        if (progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      observer.disconnect();
    }, { threshold: 0.4 });
    if (ref.current) observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [value, reduced]);

  return (
    <div ref={ref}>
      <p className="font-heading text-5xl tracking-tight tabular-nums">
        {shown}
        {suffix}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[0.65rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### AccordionShowcase

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/motion/AccordionShowcase.jsx`
- **Core features:** Expandable visual explanations
- **Purpose:** Details on demand without a second page.
- **Rule:** Disclosure semantics, and the first panel open so the section has visible content.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// AccordionShowcase — motion, interaction, and premium layers
// Details on demand without a second page.
// Rule: Disclosure semantics, and the first panel open so the section has visible content.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Expandable visual explanations
export default function AccordionShowcase({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### VideoPlayer

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/motion/VideoPlayer.jsx`
- **Core features:** Captions · transcript · poster · controls
- **Purpose:** Video with the controls and text people need.
- **Rule:** Native controls unless there is a reason not to; captions default on for silent autoplay contexts.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// VideoPlayer — motion, interaction, and premium layers
// Video with the controls and text people need.
// Rule: Native controls unless there is a reason not to; captions default on for silent autoplay contexts.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Captions · transcript · poster · controls
export default function VideoPlayer({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

```jsx
// Holds one element while a sequence scrolls past it.

// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Pinned content · progress indicator
export default function StickySection({ eyebrow, heading, intro, items = [], action }) {
  return (
    <section className="border-b border-border">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-6xl gap-10 px-5 py-16 lg:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr] lg:px-10">
        <div>
          {eyebrow ? <p className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-primary">{eyebrow}</p> : null}
          <h2 className="mt-4 font-heading text-[clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)] leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em]">{heading}</h2>
          <p className="measure mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{intro}</p>
          {action ? <a href={action.href} className="mt-6 inline-flex border border-border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm">{action.label}</a> : null}
        </div>
        <ul className="grid gap-px border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.id} className="bg-background p-5">
              <p className="font-heading text-sm">{item.title}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.detail}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### ParallaxMedia

- **Type:** Flagship
- **File:** `components/motion/ParallaxMedia.jsx`
- **Core features:** Subtle layered movement
- **Purpose:** Adds depth to a hero or a full-bleed section.
- **Rule:** Keep the offset small and use transform only; large parallax causes motion sickness and layout jank.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ParallaxMedia — motion, interaction, and premium layers
// Adds depth to a hero or a full-bleed section.
// Rule: Keep the offset small and use transform only; large parallax causes motion sickness and layout jank.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Subtle layered movement
export default function ParallaxMedia({ src, poster, alt, caption }) {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    setReduced(query.matches);
    const onChange = (event) => setReduced(event.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return (
    <figure className="overflow-hidden border border-border">
      {reduced || !src ? (
        <img src={poster} alt={alt} loading="lazy" className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <video src={src} poster={poster} controls muted playsInline className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
      )}
      {caption ? <figcaption className="border-t border-border px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{caption}</figcaption> : null}
    </figure>
  );
}
```

#### MagneticButton

- **Type:** Flagship
- **File:** `components/motion/MagneticButton.jsx`
- **Core features:** Small transform movement · disabled on touch
- **Purpose:** A tactile pull on primary actions.
- **Rule:** Twenty pixels at most, disabled on touch and under reduced motion, and never so strong that the hit area moves away from the cursor.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// MagneticButton — motion, interaction, and premium layers
// A tactile pull on primary actions.
// Rule: Twenty pixels at most, disabled on touch and under reduced motion, and never so strong that the hit area moves away from the cursor.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Small transform movement · disabled on touch
export default function MagneticButton({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### AudioAtmosphere

- **Type:** Flagship
- **File:** `components/motion/AudioAtmosphere.jsx`
- **Core features:** Muted by default · explicit toggle
- **Purpose:** Ambient sound as an opt-in layer.
- **Rule:** Never autoplay with sound, remember the choice, and give a visible stop control.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// AudioAtmosphere — motion, interaction, and premium layers
// Ambient sound as an opt-in layer.
// Rule: Never autoplay with sound, remember the choice, and give a visible stop control.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Muted by default · explicit toggle
export default function AudioAtmosphere({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
// PersonalizedRecommendations — motion, interaction, and premium layers

  );
}
```

---

## 20 · Forms, validation, and feedback

Every form needs labels, inline validation, accessible errors, success confirmation, and a privacy/consent explanation.

**Architecture**

- One schema, validated on the client for speed and on the server for truth. Share the schema rather than writing rules twice.
- Model form state explicitly: idle, dirty, submitting, succeeded, failed. Every one of those has a visible state.
- Submission must be idempotent — a double click, a refresh or a flaky connection cannot create two records.

**Accessibility**

- Programmatic labels, aria-required, aria-invalid, and aria-describedby tying hints and errors to their field.
- Error summaries link to the first invalid field and move focus there.
- Group related controls in fieldset/legend; never use placeholder text as the only label.

**Performance**

- Validate on blur, not on every keystroke, and debounce anything that calls the network.
- Upload directly to storage with progress, then submit the reference — not the bytes through your function.

**Quality gate**

- Complete every form with a screen reader and with keyboard only.
- Confirm the success state and check the record or email actually arrived.

### Forms, validation, and feedback — Starter tier (8)

#### FormField

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/forms/ContactForm.jsx`
- **Core features:** Label · hint · required state · error · aria attributes
- **Purpose:** The primitive every other field is built from.
- **Rule:** Label above, hint and error tied by id, one error at a time, and never a red border without text.
- **Code:** reference implementation (runs in the component kit) — “ContactForm — five fields, three visible states”
- **Note:** One shared schema on both sides; errors tied to their field; the success screen carries a reference.

```jsx
const schema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(2, "Tell us your name"),
  email: z.string().email("Use a valid email address"),
  phone: z.string().optional(),
  message: z.string().min(12, "A sentence or two is enough"),
  consent: z.literal(true, { errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please agree before sending" }) }),
});

function ContactForm() {
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(schema),
    mode: "onBlur",
  });
  const send = useServerFn(submitEnquiry);
  const [reference, setReference] = useState(null);

  if (reference) {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-primary/40 bg-card p-8">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-2xl">Thank you — your enquiry is in.</h2>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          We reply within one working day. Your reference is
          <span className="ml-1 font-mono text-foreground">{reference}</span>.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      onSubmit={handleSubmit(async (values) => {
        const result = await send({ data: values });
        setReference(result.reference);
      })}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      {["name", "email", "message"].map((field) => (
        <div key={field}>
          <label htmlFor={field} className="mb-2 block text-sm">
            {labels[field]}
          </label>
          <input
            id={field}
            {...register(field)}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(formState.errors[field])}
            aria-describedby={formState.errors[field] ? field + "-error" : undefined}
            className="w-full border border-input bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-sm
                       focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
          />
          {formState.errors[field] && (
            <p id={field + "-error"} role="alert" className="mt-2 text-xs text-destructive">
              {formState.errors[field].message}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}

      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <input id="consent" type="checkbox" {...register("consent")} className="mt-1" />
        <label htmlFor="consent" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          I agree to be contacted about my enquiry. See the privacy notice.
        </label>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={formState.isSubmitting}
        className="rounded-full bg-primary px-7 py-3 text-sm font-medium
                   text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {formState.isSubmitting ? "Sending…" : "Send enquiry"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### PhoneField

- **Type:** Starter
- **File:** `components/form/PhoneField.jsx`
- **Core features:** Country code · formatting · validation
- **Purpose:** Phone capture that works internationally.
- **Rule:** Store in E.164, format for display, and never reject a valid international number.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// PhoneField — forms, validation, and feedback
// Phone capture that works internationally.
// Rule: Store in E.164, format for display, and never reject a valid international number.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Country code · formatting · validation
export default function PhoneField({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### ConditionalLogic

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/form/ConditionalLogic.jsx`
- **Core features:** Show/hide fields based on prior answers
- **Purpose:** Only ask what is relevant.
- **Rule:** Hidden fields must also be excluded from validation and from the submitted payload.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// ConditionalLogic — forms, validation, and feedback
// Only ask what is relevant.
// Rule: Hidden fields must also be excluded from validation and from the submitted payload.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Show/hide fields based on prior answers
export default function ConditionalLogic({ fields = [], onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const missing = fields.filter((field) => field.required && !values[field.name]);
    if (missing.length) {
      setError("Complete " + missing.map((field) => field.label).join(", ") + ".");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setStatus("sending");
    await onSubmit(values);
    setStatus("done");
  };

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div role="status" className="border border-border p-8">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg">Received</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">The success state confirms what happens next and when.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-4 border-0 p-0">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label htmlFor={field.name} className="text-sm">
              {field.label}
              {field.required ? <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span> : null}
            </label>
            <input id={field.name} name={field.name} type={field.type || "text"} required={field.required}
              aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2"
              onChange={(event) => setValues({ ...values, [field.name]: event.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={status === "sending"} className="justify-self-start bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60">
        {status === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Submit"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

#### SurveyModule

- **Type:** Growth
- **File:** `components/form/SurveyModule.jsx`
- **Core features:** Rating · NPS · open text · branching
- **Purpose:** Structured feedback with a follow-up path.
- **Rule:** One question per screen, visible progress, and free text always optional.
- **Code:** generated scaffold — structure, props, states and keyboard behaviour; add the real copy

```jsx
// SurveyModule — forms, validation, and feedback
// Structured feedback with a follow-up path.
// Rule: One question per screen, visible progress, and free text always optional.
// A11y: keyboard reachable, visible focus, no colour-only meaning.
// Features: Rating · NPS · open text · branching
export default function SurveyModule({ label, options = [], onSelect }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const trigger = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        trigger.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <button ref={trigger} type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} className="border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        {label}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <ul role="listbox" className="absolute left-0 top-full z-20 mt-2 w-64 border border-border bg-popover p-2">
          {options.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.value}>
              <button type="button" role="option" aria-selected={option.selected}
                onClick={() => { onSelect?.(option.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus(); }}
                className="block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-secondary">
                {option.label}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Tracked events

The minimum analytics vocabulary. Each event carries the route, template, tier and one decision the business can act on — never free text or personal data.

| Event | What it tells you |
| --- | --- |
| `page_viewed` | Route and template performance |
| `cta_clicked` | Which action and location |
| `form_started` | Conversion intent |
| `form_abandoned` | Field/step friction |
| `form_submitted` | Successful conversion |
| `booking_started` | Scheduling intent |
| `booking_completed` | Confirmed appointment/reservation |
| `quote_started` | Estimate intent |
| `quote_completed` | Qualified lead |
| `product_viewed` | Commerce interest |
| `add_to_cart` | Purchase intent |
| `checkout_started` | Purchase progression |
| `purchase_completed` | Revenue event |
| `donation_started` | Fundraising intent |
| `donation_completed` | Donation conversion |
| `filter_used` | Catalogue/listing behavior |
| `search_used` | Content/findability gaps |
| `media_opened` | Gallery/video engagement |
| `portal_login` | Returning-user behavior |
| `recommendation_clicked` | AI/personalization effectiveness |
| `call_clicked` | Phone-first conversion |
| `directions_clicked` | Local intent |
| `language_changed` | Localization usage |
