## Tier matrix

What each tier is expected to provide per area. Starter must be complete, not crippled.

| Area | Starter | Growth | Business | Flagship |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Global system** | Core | Core | Core | Core |
| **Hero modules** | 1 simple | 1–2 | 2–3 | 3–4 |
| **Value/explanation** | Basic | Structured | Deep | Personalized |
| **Proof/trust** | Basic | Reviews + FAQ | Case studies + credentials | Full proof system |
| **Conversion** | Contact/call | Booking/quote/order | Multi-step intake | Portal-grade workflows |
| **Commerce** | No/simple | Simple order | Full catalogue | Accounts, loyalty, B2B |
| **Booking/operations** | CTA only | Built-in booking | Staff/resource booking | Client portal |
| **Local SEO** | NAP + map | Service areas | Multi-location | Programmatic local |
| **Content/SEO** | Basic pages | Blog + FAQ | Resources + schema | Docs/news/programmatic |
| **Motion** | Minimal | Refined | Signature | Art-directed |
| **3D/AI** | No | No | Optional isolated | Optional immersive |
| **Analytics** | Basic | Events | Full funnel | Full funnel + dashboards |
| **Multilingual** | No | Optional | Ready | Full localized journeys |

---

## Build order

The order is not a preference: each stage depends on the records and primitives established by the one before it.

### 01 · Global system

- **Includes:** header, footer, mobile nav, CTA, forms, breadcrumbs, consent, 404.
- **Why now:** Everything else is mounted inside this shell. Building it last means rebuilding every page.

### 02 · Conversion core

- **Includes:** contact, call, booking, quote, newsletter, success/error states.
- **Why now:** A page that cannot receive an enquiry is a mockup, however good it looks.

### 03 · Trust core

- **Includes:** services, process, FAQs, reviews, team, locations, gallery.
- **Why now:** These answer the questions that stop a ready visitor from converting.

### 04 · Commerce core

- **Includes:** product grid, product detail, cart, checkout, order confirmation.
- **Why now:** Build the money path end to end before adding merchandising surfaces.

### 05 · Content core

- **Includes:** blog, article, resource library, help center, careers, events.
- **Why now:** Organic entry points, and the proof of expertise a buyer checks.

### 06 · Industry modules

- **Includes:** listings, menus, courses, vehicles, tours, programs, RFQs.
- **Why now:** The domain-specific layer, built on primitives that already work.

### 07 · Portal modules

- **Includes:** dashboard, documents, bookings, invoices, status, messages.
- **Why now:** Authenticated surfaces last: they depend on every record model above.

### 08 · Premium experience

- **Includes:** scroll narrative, configurators, 3D, AI concierge, personalization.
- **Why now:** Layers on top of a correct site. Applied earlier, they hide the defects underneath.

---

## Quality gates

No module ships until all six pass. They are checked on the module itself, not on the page as a whole.

1. **Real content — Actual copy, prices and imagery — or content clearly marked as sample with DemoContentBadge.
2. **Conversion purpose — One dominant action per page, and the module visibly supports it.
3. **Mobile layout — Checked at 320px, not assumed from a desktop breakpoint. No sideways scrolling.
4. **Keyboard support — Reachable and operable with Tab, arrows, Enter and Escape. Focus is always visible.
5. **Reduced motion — Every animation has a prefers-reduced-motion branch that keeps the content.
6. **Empty and error states — Nothing found, nothing saved, request failed — each has its own designed screen.

---

## Closing note

A practical rule: build every module with real content, a conversion purpose, a mobile layout, keyboard support, reduced-motion behavior, and an empty/error state before adding any visual “elite” layer. That is what makes the system hard to replicate: not one dramatic homepage, but a complete, coherent library of business-grade components.
